        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Soul soil may be tilled to form Soul Farmland. Crops work a little differently when you are Beneath. There are five nutrients: Death, Destruction, Decay, Sorrow, and Flame. Nutrients are viewed by holding a hoe, but will not appear unless they have a value greater than zero.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Crops have a preferred nutrient: Instead of consuming it, this is the nutrient they will add to over time. The crop will happily consume the other four nutrients in order to grow. Nether crops cannot die, but still need block light to grow.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The fertilizers are as follows: Sulfur: 20% decay, 10% flame Gunpowder: 60% Destruction, 10% Flame, 40% Death Ghast Tear: 30% Sorrow Blaze Powder: 20% Flame",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The fertilizers are also available in obtainable 'pure' forms from chest loot. Crafting these together yields Agonizing Fertilizer, which has them all in a neat package.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Crimson Roots (Decay) and Warped Roots (Destruction) are growable as crops. These are most useful as means of improving the nutrient quality of your soul farmland. Nether Wart (Death), found in fortresses, can also be grown as a crop.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The Gleamflower (Flame) can be grown as a crop, and also found in the world as a plant. It is useful as an infinite light source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Ghost Peppers (Sorrow) are an edible vegetable that can be grown in the Beneath!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "gtceu:gold_bolt",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "Au",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Gold Bolt",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "gtceu:gold_bolt",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "Melts into 18 mB of Gold (at Orange٭٭٭)",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Gold Bolt",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "ore_processing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "An important part of GregTech is its Ore Processing Mechanics, but it can get pretty complicated, so this chapter is here to help!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "To start with, you should be aware of the Ore Processing Diagram tab in EMI - Press the Show Uses key (default U) on any item related to ore processing and look for the tab with the vanilla Iron Ore icon. This diagram may look overwhelming at first, but we'll take it step by step.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "A major part of GregTech philosophy is that there's not just one way to solve a problem, but we've marked everything important with a star, so if you're completely lost, try following those!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "To Process, Ore Not To Process",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "I agree to not just dump everything in a furnace",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Welcome to Ore Processing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "While looking at EMI, you've probably noticed that your Macerator or Millstone lists more outputs than what you're actually getting.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "GregTech doesn't communicate this very well, but these extra slots are only available in HV. Once you make it to that tier, the HV macerator becomes incredibly powerful, giving a huge amount of bonus byproducts from each ore.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "I can get even more from my ores!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:hv_macerator)item(greate:stainless_steel_crushing_wheel))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Either an HV Macerator or HS Crushing Wheels",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Crushing with byproducts",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Bathing certain ores in Mercury can give some other useful byproducts. Not all ores can be processed this way, but the ones that can, will get around a 70 percent chance of byproducts, compared to the 30 percent of the Ore Washer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Mercury can be easily obtained from Redstone or Cinnabar.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The quest below gives some examples of crushed ores which are good to process in Mercury.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Don't stick your finger in it",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Bathe them in Mercury",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Once you get to LV, you may be interested in using a Chemical Bath with Sodium persulfate to get some extra byproducts. Not all ores can be processed this way, but the ones that can, will get around a 70 percent chance of byproducts, compared to the 30 percent of the Ore Washer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Sodium persulfate can be a little annoying to mass produce, though if you want, Sea Water is a great starting point.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The quest below gives some examples that might be worth the investment.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Not just for circuit board etching",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Bathe them in Sodium Persulfate",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Here's some recommendations for Mercury bathing:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "- Crushed Platinum: Palladium",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "- Crushed Cooperite: Palladium",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "- Crushed Nickel: Platinum",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "- Crushed Gold: Silver",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Liquid metal bath time",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Mercury bathing examples",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Here's some recommendations for Sodium Persulfate bathing:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "- Crushed Aluminium: for Rutile (Titanium)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "- Crushed Bauxite: Gallium",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "- Crushed Cobalt: Cobaltite for Arsenic",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "A useful time saver",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Sodium Persulfate bathing examples",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Indium Line shouldn't be needed before IV, but it will require purified Sphalerite and Galena.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "We say line, but really it's just a two step process",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "only_one(item(gtceu:indium_concentrate_bucket)nbt(fuzzy:{fluid:{Amount:1000,FluidName:\"gtceu:indium_concentrate\"}}))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Indium Line",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Platinum Line will be your first real challenge to process, providing you with essential platinum-group metals: Platinum, Palladium, Ruthenium, Rhodium, Iridium, and Osmium.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "While there's easier ways to obtain Platinum and Palladium, this line will be mandatory for accessing the others.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Platline can be started at HV, but you won’t be able to complete it fully until later in EV.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "GregTech players vs Mekanism players when they need Osmium:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "ftbfiltersystem:item_tag(tfg:platinum_ore_group)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Any #tfg:platinum_ore_group",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You’ve built new chemistry lines, manufactured advanced alloys, and even launched a Rocket to reach the Moon. We hope you enjoyed the ride, because now things get even more complex, with powerful new machines and demanding processes ahead.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "In the EV Chapter, your main objective will be to craft your very first IV and LuV Circuits.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Along the way, several key challenges await you:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "• Build your first Alloy Blast Smelter, a faster version of the EBF, specialized for alloys. With it, you’ll be able to construct your first Large Machine: the Large Centrifuge, essential for the Nuclear Fission Line. It also lets you upgrade ore processing with a faster Thermal Centrifuge.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "• Master our original Tungsten Line - looping all the required materials will test both your knowledge and your patience.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "• Prepare to fly to Mars where you’ll become an extraterrestrial farmer while also learning to harness the power of the atom.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Good luck, this is where GregTech begins to show its true depth!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Let's go Nuclear on Mars",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "This is Extreme Voltage",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "This new rocket shouldn’t be too difficult to assemble, but you’ll notice a clear jump in material costs compared to your previous model.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The upgraded Rocket Alloys now require both Distilled Water and Cryogenized Fluix for cooling.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Make sure you've fully set up your Titanium production line, as you're going to need a lot of it going forward! A continuous production line will save you a lot of frustration down the road.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "This one's purple",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(ad_astra:tier_2_rocket)item(tfg:tier_2_double_rocket))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Rocket 2: Titanium Boogaloo",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "First, like the Moon, Mars will have some hostile surface mobs as well. If you haven't already figured out how to make your Space Suit stay with you after dying or invested in new EV-tier weaponry, you'll have a rough time.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The planet is mostly a desert world, dotted with \"islands\" where you’ll find water, lush fauna, and much safer places to build your first base. Meanwhile, the open desert is extremely dangerous if you don't watch your step, but contains some other crucial resources!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "All fluid veins are spread across Mars, except for Heavy Ammoniacial Water which can only be found outside of the desert. That’s another good reason to set up your base there.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Energy-wise, Mars will not be kind to you at first.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Nearly every common energy generation method is disabled or worthless on the planet, which means you’ll have to rely almost entirely on Fission to power your base.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "At the very beginning, we recommend bringing along some filled batteries such as Lapotron Crystals to get started, and then look towards setting up Thorium Fuel Rods.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Achieving energy stability on Mars will take time and effort, but once your systems are running smoothly, you’ll feel like unlimited power is right in the palm of your hand.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "And one last tip: don’t kill everything you see. Some animals can be ranched, and you’ll definitely need them later in your progression.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Be sure to check out the Space Survival chapter for more Mars-related tips too!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Stay alert, plan ahead, and Mars will reward your courage.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Red Planet",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Land on Mars",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "I'm on Mars!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Congrats on making your first Tungsten! We hope you enjoy this small increase in complexity. Remember that, at first, the small amount of Tungsten you require can be achieved by simply inputting the Soda Ash you mined. This may inspire you to fully loop the process so you can automate it peacefully.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Also remember that the further you progress, the more important it will be to build dedicated and passive lines for all these complex processes that will be required until the end of the pack. Don’t be afraid of lacking resources, be afraid of lacking time.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Fully self-sustaining, just like Titanium",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Tungsten Line in TerraFirmaGreg has been completely reworked and you’ll be happy (or not) to know that it’s now much harder.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "It’s possible to fully loop every material in the Tungsten Line, but doing so requires a lot of engineering. Alternatively, you can brute-force your way through by mass-producing the resources you need.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "A little tip: Mars has several good ore veins for Soda Ash and Tungsten.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Take your time, optimize, and the Tungsten Line will become the foundation of your mid-to-late game progress.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Why? Just why?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The new Tungsten Line",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "all_started",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You know what to do with this by now, but this is a good time to give you a heads up that now is the time to scale up your factory if you haven't already done so.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Take your time to set up AE2 all over your base, making it print you dozens of new machines to keep your factory running at tip-top shape, because if you don't, the rest of EV is going to hit you like a brick wall.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Don't wait to find out the hard way!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "At the very least, here's what we recommend before pushing further into EV:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- Passive production of Rubber, Polyethylene, Oxygen, Hydrogen, Nitrogen, and Chlorine",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- At least two general-purpose EBFs, and a few LCRs",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- AE2 patterns for a circuit of every tier and most of the common crafting components like motors, pumps, plates, and wires",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- A dedicated ore processing setup",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- A couple EV amps worth of power generation (see the below quests)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You can do it, you've made it this far already!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Last step before some Extreme power generation!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "EV Assembler",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Distillation Tower (DT) is a direct multiblock upgrade of the Distillery. You won't have to choose outputs any more as the DT will handle them all automatically, at an increased power cost.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "This machine will be your future bread and butter for anything involving Petrochem or Organic distillation.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Like the Cleanroom, the DT can be made taller, though not wider. Starting from the second layer, each added level requires an Output Hatch and grants one additional fluid output slot; if a level lacks its output hatch, the corresponding fluid will be voided. Count the number of outputs with EMI so you know how many layers you need. As always, use the Multiblock Preview tab in EMI and click the multiblock to see where you can place each part.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Note: Be careful not to over-overclock the DT when producing fuels! Excessive overclocking can reduce the overall power yield, especially for Diesel. Do the math and double-check your calculations.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ev__extreme_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": [],
      "description": "Candidate player storage section inferred from pack evidence; accept only if it is a useful way to group items."
    },

        {

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Soul soil may be tilled to form Soul Farmland. Crops work a little differently when you are Beneath. There are five nutrients: Death, Destruction, Decay, Sorrow, and Flame. Nutrients are viewed by holding a hoe, but will not appear unless they have a value greater than zero.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Crops have a preferred nutrient: Instead of consuming it, this is the nutrient they will add to over time. The crop will happily consume the other four nutrients in order to grow. Nether crops cannot die, but still need block light to grow.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The fertilizers are as follows: Sulfur: 20% decay, 10% flame Gunpowder: 60% Destruction, 10% Flame, 40% Death Ghast Tear: 30% Sorrow Blaze Powder: 20% Flame",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The fertilizers are also available in obtainable 'pure' forms from chest loot. Crafting these together yields Agonizing Fertilizer, which has them all in a neat package.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Crimson Roots (Decay) and Warped Roots (Destruction) are growable as crops. These are most useful as means of improving the nutrient quality of your soul farmland. Nether Wart (Death), found in fortresses, can also be grown as a crop.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The Gleamflower (Flame) can be grown as a crop, and also found in the world as a plant. It is useful as an infinite light source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Ghost Peppers (Sorrow) are an edible vegetable that can be grown in the Beneath!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },

      "id": "pack:tfg/contraption_controlled",
      "label": "Contraption Controlled",

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "create:funnel",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!data/create/advancements/funnel.json",
          "text": "Airport Aesthetic",
          "key": "advancement-title:title",
          "label": "Airport Aesthetic",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "create:funnel",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!data/create/advancements/funnel.json",
          "text": "Extract or insert items into a container using a Funnel",
          "key": "advancement-description:description",
          "label": "Airport Aesthetic",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "questssteam_age",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "The Millstone is an automatic version of the Quern. You can throw whatever you'd like into the top, and then right-click to take your crushed items back out. It's pretty slow if you connect it directly to your Animal Crank, but you can use gear ratios to increase its speed.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "You will only receive the output in the first slot. The other slots are part of a GregTech mechanic that won't be relevant until much later (HV).",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: The millstone can't be used to process grains into flour because Create doesn't understand TFC's food expiry system, which previously led to all sorts of bugs involving rotten items becoming fresh and vice versa. Until you're able to get the Food Processor in LV, you can still crush your grains via a Mortar in a crafting grid.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "No more querning",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Automatic Ore Processing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "The Animal Crank is your first accessible source of mechanical power. To use it, place the crank on the center of a 7x7 cleared area and leash an animal to it. Different animals will provide different amounts of power, while the blocks underneath will increase the speed of the output power. The area of multiple cranks can overlap.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "You may need to hold a second lead to attach an animal.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Small Animals (16 SU):",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Wolf, Dog, Pig, Sheep, Goat, Alpaca",
          "key": "quest-snbt",

          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Horse, Mule, Musk Ox, Yak",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
