          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Make your livestock pay rent",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Your First Power Source",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Does crafting all those plates for a Bloomery look tedious? The Helve Hammer will automatically (but slowly) flatten double ingots into plates for you. You'll still have to do the welding yourself though. Simply place an anvil directly under the hammer and throw your double ingots onto it by pressing the Throw Item key (Default: Q), then right-click the hammer to get your plates out.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Higher tier anvils will also require fewer hits to make plates from the previous tier.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Be sure to use cogs to increase its speed!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "It may be unimpressive to start, but once you're able to increase its speed to 256 RPM with Steel cogs and shafts, the Helve Hammer will really start to shine.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "CLANG... CLANG... CLANG...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Helve Hammer",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Greate is a fusion of Create and GregTech that has different tiered versions of Create's machines. It also introduces stress limits on Shafts and Cogwheels, so you'll have to plan more about how you power your machinery!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Generally speaking, you'll want smaller setups with their own individual power sources, instead of doing the usual Create thing of having a blob of water wheels connected into a single Shaft into your base.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Tired of Create? This'll make it more interesting",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Basics of Greate",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Water wheels provide much more power than the Animal Crank, at the expense of requiring flowing water. You don't have the ability to move water sources yet, so you'll need to find a river to power these. Oceans and Lakes can also provide a little power, but if you're far inland, you're better off looking at building a windmill.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "You can get treated wood by soaking any planks in creosote from your Coke Oven.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Hope you set up your base near a river!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Water Wheels",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "If you aren't near flowing water, a windmill is another effective option for a better source of power. Bigger windmills provide more stress units and faster speeds.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Pairs well with tulips",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "These are the basis of most of the simple machines you can build with Create. Check JEI for all the different things you can craft with this, including Gearboxes and other ways to control your stress units.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "New to Create? There's many tutorials online about different kinds of contraptions you can build, and most of them will still work in TFG too! (Except anything involving cobble generators)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Primitive Machine Hull?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Metal Casings",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "The Encased Fan is the final step in your first fully automatic ore processing line, so you no longer need to clean your impure dusts by hand.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Greate's Encased Fans work a little differently than in base Create. Instead of placing a water source in front of the fan, you'll need to pump water into the encased fan block itself. Be sure to set the number on the fan to 1!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Next, place your crushed ore or dusts on a Depot or Belt in front of the fan (the floor will NOT work!), and they'll be gleaming in no time. As a bonus, it'll give you some extra byproducts too!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Great for hot days",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Encased Fan",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "With the Steel Age, now you can automate fluids as well as items! The Mechanical Pump can pull fluids out of pretty much anything, including barrels, crucibles, and even source blocks. You can use it with either Create's or GregTech's fluid pipes, though each behaves a little differently.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Create's pipes have no internal fluid capacity, instead transporting fluids directly from source to destination. You can right-click them with a Copper Casing to \"lock\" any connections in place, and right-clicking them with a wrench will open a little window that prevents intersections, so you can run multiple pipes next to each other. Create's pipes also require a mechanical pump to use, and each pump only has a range of 16 blocks, but one pump can pull fluids from multiple sources at once.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "They also have no temperature limit!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "On the other hand, GregTech's pipes do have their own internal fluid capacity and don't require a pump once fluid is inside them. Like item pipes, their connections can also be fully controlled by sneak right-clicking them with an empty off-hand, and we recommend doing so on all your pipes otherwise the fluid sloshes around.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Are your mechanical pumps and GregTech pipes not connecting? Flip the pump twice (right-click with a wrench) and that should get them working together.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Delicious pipe spaghetti",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Fluid Handling",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Steam machines need to be powered! You have three options to generate steam: the Solid boiler accepts solid fuels like coal and charcoal, the Liquid boiler accepts liquids like creosote and lava, and the Solar boiler requires no fuel but only works during the day. The choice is yours, but we recommend the liquid one if you're unsure.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Steam machines all have a vent which needs to stay unblocked in order for them to work properly.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Warning: Boilers will explode if you attempt to put water into them while they are dry and hot. It's best to always have water being pumped into them, but you can always break and place the block to reset its temperature.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Portable Steamroom",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:hp_steam_solid_boiler)item(gtceu:hp_steam_liquid_boiler)item(gtceu:hp_steam_solar_boiler))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Any High Pressure Steam Boiler",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Steam Boilers",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Water wheels and windmills not cutting it any more? It wouldn't be a steam age without a proper steam engine! Each steam engine can output a whole 256 SU, giving you much more power to play with.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Blaze Burners can be crafted without leaving your house, and you can use a Mechanical Arm to automatically feed them solid fuel, or directly pipe liquid fuel (such as creosote). Coal Gems, Anthracite, and Coke can also be used to superheat them.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Time for some real steam power",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Steam Engine",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Tired of chopping trees? These will do it for you! Place them on a spinning contraption and watch them go. Unfortunately, you'll still have to replant the saplings them yourself until you get access to a Deployer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "A Mechanical Saw can also double as a stonecutter, giving easier access to various forms of masonry. Using it to chop logs into lumber is also more efficient!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": [],
      "description": "Candidate player storage section inferred from pack evidence; accept only if it is a useful way to group items."
    },

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "ore_processing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "An important part of GregTech is its Ore Processing Mechanics, but it can get pretty complicated, so this chapter is here to help!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "To start with, you should be aware of the Ore Processing Diagram tab in EMI - Press the Show Uses key (default U) on any item related to ore processing and look for the tab with the vanilla Iron Ore icon. This diagram may look overwhelming at first, but we'll take it step by step.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "A major part of GregTech philosophy is that there's not just one way to solve a problem, but we've marked everything important with a star, so if you're completely lost, try following those!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "To Process, Ore Not To Process",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "I agree to not just dump everything in a furnace",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Welcome to Ore Processing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "While looking at EMI, you've probably noticed that your Macerator or Millstone lists more outputs than what you're actually getting.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "GregTech doesn't communicate this very well, but these extra slots are only available in HV. Once you make it to that tier, the HV macerator becomes incredibly powerful, giving a huge amount of bonus byproducts from each ore.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "I can get even more from my ores!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:hv_macerator)item(greate:stainless_steel_crushing_wheel))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Either an HV Macerator or HS Crushing Wheels",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Crushing with byproducts",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Bathing certain ores in Mercury can give some other useful byproducts. Not all ores can be processed this way, but the ones that can, will get around a 70 percent chance of byproducts, compared to the 30 percent of the Ore Washer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Mercury can be easily obtained from Redstone or Cinnabar.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The quest below gives some examples of crushed ores which are good to process in Mercury.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Don't stick your finger in it",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Bathe them in Mercury",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Once you get to LV, you may be interested in using a Chemical Bath with Sodium persulfate to get some extra byproducts. Not all ores can be processed this way, but the ones that can, will get around a 70 percent chance of byproducts, compared to the 30 percent of the Ore Washer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Sodium persulfate can be a little annoying to mass produce, though if you want, Sea Water is a great starting point.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The quest below gives some examples that might be worth the investment.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Not just for circuit board etching",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Bathe them in Sodium Persulfate",

          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",

        {

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Indium Line shouldn't be needed before IV, but it will require purified Sphalerite and Galena.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "We say line, but really it's just a two step process",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "only_one(item(gtceu:indium_concentrate_bucket)nbt(fuzzy:{fluid:{Amount:1000,FluidName:\"gtceu:indium_concentrate\"}}))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Indium Line",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Platinum Line will be your first real challenge to process, providing you with essential platinum-group metals: Platinum, Palladium, Ruthenium, Rhodium, Iridium, and Osmium.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "ore_processing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "An important part of GregTech is its Ore Processing Mechanics, but it can get pretty complicated, so this chapter is here to help!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "To start with, you should be aware of the Ore Processing Diagram tab in EMI - Press the Show Uses key (default U) on any item related to ore processing and look for the tab with the vanilla Iron Ore icon. This diagram may look overwhelming at first, but we'll take it step by step.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "A major part of GregTech philosophy is that there's not just one way to solve a problem, but we've marked everything important with a star, so if you're completely lost, try following those!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "To Process, Ore Not To Process",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "I agree to not just dump everything in a furnace",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Welcome to Ore Processing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "While looking at EMI, you've probably noticed that your Macerator or Millstone lists more outputs than what you're actually getting.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "GregTech doesn't communicate this very well, but these extra slots are only available in HV. Once you make it to that tier, the HV macerator becomes incredibly powerful, giving a huge amount of bonus byproducts from each ore.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "I can get even more from my ores!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:hv_macerator)item(greate:stainless_steel_crushing_wheel))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Either an HV Macerator or HS Crushing Wheels",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Crushing with byproducts",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Bathing certain ores in Mercury can give some other useful byproducts. Not all ores can be processed this way, but the ones that can, will get around a 70 percent chance of byproducts, compared to the 30 percent of the Ore Washer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Mercury can be easily obtained from Redstone or Cinnabar.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The quest below gives some examples of crushed ores which are good to process in Mercury.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Don't stick your finger in it",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Bathe them in Mercury",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Once you get to LV, you may be interested in using a Chemical Bath with Sodium persulfate to get some extra byproducts. Not all ores can be processed this way, but the ones that can, will get around a 70 percent chance of byproducts, compared to the 30 percent of the Ore Washer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Sodium persulfate can be a little annoying to mass produce, though if you want, Sea Water is a great starting point.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The quest below gives some examples that might be worth the investment.",

        {

          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",

          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Indium Line shouldn't be needed before IV, but it will require purified Sphalerite and Galena.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "We say line, but really it's just a two step process",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "only_one(item(gtceu:indium_concentrate_bucket)nbt(fuzzy:{fluid:{Amount:1000,FluidName:\"gtceu:indium_concentrate\"}}))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Indium Line",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Platinum Line will be your first real challenge to process, providing you with essential platinum-group metals: Platinum, Palladium, Ruthenium, Rhodium, Iridium, and Osmium.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "While there's easier ways to obtain Platinum and Palladium, this line will be mandatory for accessing the others.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Platline can be started at HV, but you won’t be able to complete it fully until later in EV.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "GregTech players vs Mekanism players when they need Osmium:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",

        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",

          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

          "text": "one_completed",

          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "For all your other Purified Ores that doesn't need special treatment, it's best to run them through the Thermal Centrifuge. This machine can produce byproducts at a reliable rate, without needing any other ingredients. It's quite slow, however, so we suggest either making multiple machines or the steam parallel multiblock.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The go-to for most purified ores",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:lv_thermal_centrifuge)item(gtceu:mv_thermal_centrifuge)item(gtceu:hv_thermal_centrifuge)item(gtceu:ev_thermal_centrifuge)item(gtceu:iv_thermal_centrifuge)item(gtceu:luv_thermal_centrifuge)item(gtceu:zpm_thermal_centrifuge)item(gtceu:uv_thermal_centrifuge)item(tfg:steam_thermal_centrifuge))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Any Thermal Centrifuge",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Thermal Centrifuge",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Sifter should be used for all your Gems. It gives far more yield per ore than any other method, and is the only way to obtain Exquisite and Flawless gems before HV laser engraving.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Skyblock Simulator",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",

          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Sifter",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Sifter can output Purified Dust, which you can toss into a centrifuge for a few extra byproducts.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",

          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Centrifuging the dust",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Electromagnetic Separator can extract things like Iron and Gold from Refined Ores, which you can get by crushing your purified ones. For now, we recommend using it primarily for Naquadah. If you need more Neodymium, you can also process Bastnasite.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",

          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "sns:tfc/story/frame_pack",
          "source": "jar:Sacks 'N Such-1.20.1-1.2.7.jar!data/sns/advancements/tfc/story/frame_pack.json",
          "text": "Un-Sophisticated Backpack",

        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "sns:tfc/story/frame_pack",
          "source": "jar:Sacks 'N Such-1.20.1-1.2.7.jar!data/sns/advancements/tfc/story/frame_pack.json",
          "text": "Use steel to create a Frame Pack. How Sophisticated?",
          "key": "advancement-description:description",
          "label": "Un-Sophisticated Backpack",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/sns/frame_pack",
          "source": "jar:Sacks 'N Such-1.20.1-1.2.7.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/sns/frame_pack.json",
          "text": "The Frame Pack",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "The Frame Pack",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/sns/frame_pack",
          "source": "jar:Sacks 'N Such-1.20.1-1.2.7.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/sns/frame_pack.json",
          "text": "The Frame Pack is a type of Item Container, essentially a TFC chest on your back. Like the TFC chest it respects TFC's Size system.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "The Frame Pack",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/sns/frame_pack",
          "source": "jar:Sacks 'N Such-1.20.1-1.2.7.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/sns/frame_pack.json",
          "text": "By default these can hold up to Large Items",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "The Frame Pack",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",

          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "Slot: Back",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",

          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "sns:frame_pack",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "Hold (Shift) for container info",

        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/sns/quiver",
          "source": "jar:Sacks 'N Such-1.20.1-1.2.7.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/sns/quiver.json",
          "text": "The Quiver is a type of Item Container every ranged warriors best friend, be it one with love for the Javalin or those who prefer the bow. Quivers will be drawn from first for \"ammo\", when using the bow and will replace thrown Javelins.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Quiver",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/sns/quiver",
          "source": "jar:Sacks 'N Such-1.20.1-1.2.7.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/sns/quiver.json",
          "text": "For modpack authors or simply people wishing to support extra \"ammo\" see the item tag sns:allowed_in_quiver. Do note the Javalin refill will not work with anything but TFC (or addon) Javelins",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Quiver",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },

          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Whether you decide to set up a miner or manually dig out a full vein, make sure to gather a hefty amount, as you'll need it to progress through AE2.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "One small step for you, one giant leap for logistics",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:poor_raw_certus_quartz)item(gtceu:raw_certus_quartz)item(gtceu:rich_raw_certus_quartz))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Certus Quartz Ore",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Certus Quartz",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "One of your main resources for Applied Energistics 2 will be Charged Certus Quartz.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "To obtain it, there’s really only one method: the HV Polarizer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "You’ll quickly notice how long this recipe takes, so it’s strongly recommended to dedicate a Polarizer exclusively to this task.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "It may seem expensive or daunting at first, but don't worry—it’s absolutely worth it.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Also, remember not to charge all your Certus Quartz!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "You’ll need some uncharged ones for items like the Annihilation Core, so always keep a small reserve on hand.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Redstone isn't enough",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "The Sifter is used for ore processing to increase your yield of Certus Quartz, Quartzite, Nether Quartz, Ruby, and Armalcolite—all of which are essential for AE2. Alternatively, you can smelt the ores directly in a Furnace if you're in a rush, but you'll lose out on a lot of free resources by doing that.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "The second machine, the Mixer, is your only method to create Fluix Crystals (more about that in the next quest). Don’t forget that you can also use the Greate Mixer as a HS-tier machine.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Thirdly, a Laser Engraver can combine lesser quality gems together into Exquisite, which will give you better Fluix yields.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Lastly, keep in mind that an Autoclave can help you generate even more gems from dusts. Once you have the resources, crafting one will make your life significantly easier.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Or is it four?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:hv_mixer)item(greate:stainless_steel_mechanical_mixer))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Greate or GregTech?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(vintageimprovements:vibrating_table)item(gtceu:lv_sifter)item(gtceu:mv_sifter)item(gtceu:hv_sifter)item(gtceu:ev_sifter)item(gtceu:iv_sifter)item(gtceu:luv_sifter)item(gtceu:zpm_sifter)item(gtceu:uv_sifter))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Any Sifter",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "The Three Musketeers",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "As you can see, there are multiple ways to create Fluix Crystals using a Mixer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "The higher the purity of your input gems, the better the yield — that’s exactly why we recommended crafting a Sifter earlier.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "You’ll need a massive amount of Fluix Crystals, especially since the liquid version is used in most Applied Energistics 2 recipes.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "So prepare yourself—you’re going to need a lot!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Energetic Crystal",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Most of your Fluix Crystals will be converted into Liquid Fluix.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "However, make sure to keep a few in their crystal form for the occasional recipe that specifically requires it.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Any Extractor will do the job here — no need for anything fancy.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "The precious fluix",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:lv_extractor)item(gtceu:mv_extractor)item(gtceu:hv_extractor)item(gtceu:ev_extractor))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Any Extractor",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Fluid Extractor",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "We told you you'd need a vast quantity of Liquid Fluix… but we didn’t mention there’s a way to drastically increase your yield per Fluix Crystal.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Introducing: Cryogenized Fluix, an ultra-cold fluid only craftable on the Moon using a Vacuum Freezer. Simply combine Liquid Fluix with Helium-3!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "This special fluid can be used as a substitute in nearly all recipes that require Liquid Fluix—but only when you're crafting on the Moon.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Moon-based AE2 recipes come with huge benefits: reduced energy costs, less components needed, or sometimes both! Investing in a proper Moon base for AE2 production will pay off massively, especially in early game.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "It’s not strictly mandatory — yet. But setting up a second base now will ease your future progression, as planet-based infrastructure becomes essential later on. So… why not get ahead of the curve?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "The Moon is so cheap",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Liquid Fluix",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Here you are, ready to begin your AE2 infrastructure!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "This chapter will mainly guide you through what you can and cannot craft yet.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "We’ll also highlight the most important items you should focus on.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "However, to fully understand Applied Energistics 2, we strongly recommend using the G keybind (similar to Create's Ponder system) or open the ME Guide we provide as a reward from this quest. It’s the best in-game tutorial for AE2.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Alternatively, watch some online videos about AE2 on 1.20.1 for more detailed explanations — AE2 is a very popular mod, so there's a ton of tutorials out there!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Pro tip: Many AE2 recipes have alternative Moon recipes that are much cheaper. Once again, investing in your Moon base will greatly reward you throughout the entire game.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Another free book for your collection!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "Welcome to AE2",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "To get started, you’ll need to power your ME Network. These are the two AE2 blocks that can directly accept EU energy.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/applied_energistics_2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics_2.snbt",
          "text": "We strongly recommend crafting a ME Controller, as channels are enabled in TerraFirmaGreg, and the ME Controller is the best way to manage multiple channels efficiently.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "applied_energistics_2",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": [],
      "description": "Candidate player storage section inferred from pack evidence; accept only if it is a useful way to group items."
    },

        {

          "text": "Create a netherite space suit, allowing you to survive in extreme heat",
