          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Helium 3",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Lithium is the best electrolyte for LV, MV, and HV batteries.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It is abundant in Tungstate and Scheelite veins. The tungsten line yields lithium as a byproduct.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.chemical_elements.element} #3",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Beryllium is widely used for nuclear fission, including fissile fuel rods, Heat Batteries, and fuels such as FLiBe. It is also used in HV to create Ender Dust.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It can be obtained from emerald veins.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.chemical_elements.element} #4",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Boron is a component of borosilicate glass used in MV+ to make gem lenses and in HV+ to make AE2 cables, Vitrified Ender Pearls, and Fiber-Reinforced Circuit Boards.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It can be obtained in MV from Borax.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.chemical_elements.element} #5",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Carbon is the base of organic chemistry. It is used in LV+ to make resistors and in HV+ to make carbon fibers.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It can be obtained in LV in large amounts from diamond and in MV in infinite amounts from oil and tar distillation.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.chemical_elements.element} #6",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Nitrogen is used in LV+ for countless chemical reactions and to speed up the EBF and pyrolyse oven.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It can be obtained in LV in infinite amounts from air centrifuging",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.chemical_elements.element} #7",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "or(nbt(fuzzy:{fluid:{FluidName:\"gtceu:nitrogen\"}})nbt(fuzzy:{Fluid:{FluidName:\"gtceu:nitrogen\"}})item(gtceu:nitrogen_bucket))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Oxygen is used in LV+ for countless chemical reactions and to speed up the EBF.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It can be obtained in LV in infinite amounts from air centrifuging, water electrolysis, and the extremely potent flint dust to silicon dioxide processing.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.chemical_elements.element} #8",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "or(nbt(fuzzy:{fluid:{FluidName:\"gtceu:oxygen\"}})nbt(fuzzy:{Fluid:{FluidName:\"gtceu:oxygen\"}})item(gtceu:oxygen_bucket))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Fluorine is used in HV+ for polytetrafluoroethylene.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It can be obtained in MV from electrolyzing various ores such as Topaz. Unlike other gaseous elements, fluorine cannot be obtained in infinite amounts, but Topaz and Bastnatite is fluorine-rich and abundant on the Moon.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.chemical_elements.element} #9",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "or(nbt(fuzzy:{fluid:{FluidName:\"gtceu:fluorine\"}})nbt(fuzzy:{Fluid:{FluidName:\"gtceu:fluorine\"}})item(gtceu:fluorine_bucket))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Neon is used to speed up the EBF.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It can be obtained in EV+ in infinite amounts from Liquid Mars Air distillation for now, though this may change later.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.chemical_elements.element} #10",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "or(nbt(fuzzy:{fluid:{FluidName:\"gtceu:neon\"}})nbt(fuzzy:{Fluid:{FluidName:\"gtceu:neon\"}})item(gtceu:neon_bucket))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Sodium is used as a subpar LV to HV battery electrolyte and in MV+ for a small number of chemical reactions, especially when combined with chlorine to make salt.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It can be obtained in LV in infinite amounts from salt water electrolysis.",

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The UHPIC unlocks ZPM Energy Hatches and Dynamos, as well as the Active Transformer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You may know the drill, but do you know the screwdriver?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Ultra High Power Integrated Circuit",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Dielectric PCB Coolant is used to maintain some highly advanced late and end-game machinery.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You only need a little bit in LuV, but you'll want to automate it when you get to ZPM.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Absolutely beneficial for the environment",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Dielectric PCB Coolant",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Active Transformer is a multiblock used to transform energy on a much larger scale. For example, you can convert HV to LV power directly by using the apropriate energy hatches.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "It also has a second function, detailed in the above quest.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: This is yet another feature from TecTech, which also required these cables to be painted. They do look fantastic if you paint them, but it's optional in CEu.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "More than a large transformer",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Active Transformer",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Data Bank is a multiblock capable of storing huge amounts of data. You can fill it with Data Access Hatches, and fill those with Data Storage Items.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "After that, you'll need power, and then a way to connect it to your Assembly Line! This is done through Optical Fiber Cables, which can be used to link Optical Data Hatches together.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "An Optical Transmission Hatch is used to send data (like an output), and an Optical Reception Hatch to recieve the data (like an input).",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Optical cables have some restrictions however. They can only connect one input to one output. The Data Bank is your answer to this, since you can place many hatches on them to route your data anywhere.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You can use this behavior to chain Data Banks as well. Connecting one Data Bank to another, and then one of them to an Assembly Line will provide the data of both banks!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Note: This multiblock will be required later on in progression, but is very useful to set up early.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: This multiblock also originates from TecTech, but part of its mechanics required optical cables to be painted as well. You don't have to do that here, but it looks really nice if you do!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Time for a server room",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Mass Data Storage",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Active Transformer has a second use - Laser Power. Lasers are like superconductors, but behave slightly differently. They share the energy input across all connected active transformers, allowing widespread power distribution. You can use Active Transformers to convert regular EU to and from Lasers.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Like optical cables, they can only be connected to one input and output. But, these ones also only can move in straight lines. To turn them, an Active Transformer with a Laser Source Hatch (output) and Target Hatch (input) is needed.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: This is yet again another TecTech feature, but there's a bit more to it this time. TecTech's lasers were tiered by voltage and carried thousands of amps instead. CEu doesn't faff around with any of that tiering. You only need one type of laser here!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Time for some lasers!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Laser Power Transmission",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Doping Silicon with Naquadah will give a better Silicon Boule.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Use it for higher yield on Wafers.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "This will also unlock the best recipe for HV Circuits in the LuV Circuit Assembler. You're probably going to want to mass produce these for the many Lapotronic Energy Orbs that'll be required moving forwards.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Doper than ever before",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naquadah-doped Silicon Boule",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naquadah is a late-game material with a variety of uses. It is required when building a Fusion Reactor and progressing to ZPM, to name a few.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "For some reason, its fluid is a brilliant green - don't ask us why.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: Naquadah is a material originally from Stargate.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Don't you mean Stargatium?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naquadah Coils crank up the Heat Capacity to 7,200K.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "These coils are required for progressing to the second half of LuV and beyond.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: Well... not some \"juicy GregTech lore\", but a cool space fact, owing to the fact you're beating the Sun's temperature with your EBF. The surface temperature of the Sun is 5,800K. We're taking this as an effective temperature - i.e. the radiative temperature of the Sun as if it were a perfectly \"black body\". Check out the Stefan-Boltzmann law if you're interested in some crude astrophysics.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Oh, and the \"photosphere\" (the part of the sun we're talking about when we mention its surface)? It's 400 kilometres thick. That's 250 miles. Damn!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Hotter than the surface temperature of the Sun!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naquadah Coils",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "LuV Superconductors are necessary if you're eyeing a Fusion Reactor.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Don't ignore our warning here - you're gonna need a lot of them. Luckily, any Indium shortages will be solved by your Naquadah line.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Be glad these don't use pumps",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "LuV Superconductors",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Warning: Plasmas are extremely hot, with temperatures exceeding 30k Kelvin!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "However, this doesn't mean that a special type of pipe is required to transport plasma. Plasmas are treated slightly differently, and bend the rules a bit.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Fluid pipes that can handle plasmas, such as Tungsten, Naquadah, or Duranium can hold any plasma regardless of temperature. This means they won't melt under the heat of the plasma!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "WARNING: Plasmas in incompatible pipes will cause voiding, pipe burning, and disproportionately high rates of death. Be careful!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: This mechanic originates from GT6, just like the acids!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The stars are calling...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Fluid of the Stars",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Use this turbine to extract very large amounts of power from plasma!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Helium Plasma is the best fuel for its cost.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "If you're the type to question how realistic Plasma Turbines are... look up Magnetohydrodynamic Generators if you want something that bends fact into fiction.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Plasma? Turbine?! How?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Large Plasma Turbine",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "This is the main material for ZPM. Having this in stock will be a test of your Platinum processing, so this is a pre-emptive warning to stay on top of mining PGS-bearing ore.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Prepare to make lots... after you make Fusion and Crystal Circuits. Tackle those before you try making this.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Stargate's Strongest",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naquadah Alloy",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Refining Naquadah into further products will be your next automation challenge. The primary outputs of this line are Enriched Naquadah, Naquadria, and Trinium.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": [],
      "description": "IV voltage-tier components and machine parts."
    },

        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The UHPIC unlocks ZPM Energy Hatches and Dynamos, as well as the Active Transformer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You may know the drill, but do you know the screwdriver?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Ultra High Power Integrated Circuit",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Dielectric PCB Coolant is used to maintain some highly advanced late and end-game machinery.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You only need a little bit in LuV, but you'll want to automate it when you get to ZPM.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Absolutely beneficial for the environment",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Dielectric PCB Coolant",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Active Transformer is a multiblock used to transform energy on a much larger scale. For example, you can convert HV to LV power directly by using the apropriate energy hatches.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "It also has a second function, detailed in the above quest.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: This is yet another feature from TecTech, which also required these cables to be painted. They do look fantastic if you paint them, but it's optional in CEu.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "More than a large transformer",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Active Transformer",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Data Bank is a multiblock capable of storing huge amounts of data. You can fill it with Data Access Hatches, and fill those with Data Storage Items.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "After that, you'll need power, and then a way to connect it to your Assembly Line! This is done through Optical Fiber Cables, which can be used to link Optical Data Hatches together.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "An Optical Transmission Hatch is used to send data (like an output), and an Optical Reception Hatch to recieve the data (like an input).",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Optical cables have some restrictions however. They can only connect one input to one output. The Data Bank is your answer to this, since you can place many hatches on them to route your data anywhere.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You can use this behavior to chain Data Banks as well. Connecting one Data Bank to another, and then one of them to an Assembly Line will provide the data of both banks!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Note: This multiblock will be required later on in progression, but is very useful to set up early.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: This multiblock also originates from TecTech, but part of its mechanics required optical cables to be painted as well. You don't have to do that here, but it looks really nice if you do!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Time for a server room",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Mass Data Storage",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Active Transformer has a second use - Laser Power. Lasers are like superconductors, but behave slightly differently. They share the energy input across all connected active transformers, allowing widespread power distribution. You can use Active Transformers to convert regular EU to and from Lasers.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Like optical cables, they can only be connected to one input and output. But, these ones also only can move in straight lines. To turn them, an Active Transformer with a Laser Source Hatch (output) and Target Hatch (input) is needed.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: This is yet again another TecTech feature, but there's a bit more to it this time. TecTech's lasers were tiered by voltage and carried thousands of amps instead. CEu doesn't faff around with any of that tiering. You only need one type of laser here!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Time for some lasers!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Laser Power Transmission",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Doping Silicon with Naquadah will give a better Silicon Boule.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Use it for higher yield on Wafers.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "This will also unlock the best recipe for HV Circuits in the LuV Circuit Assembler. You're probably going to want to mass produce these for the many Lapotronic Energy Orbs that'll be required moving forwards.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Doper than ever before",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naquadah-doped Silicon Boule",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naquadah is a late-game material with a variety of uses. It is required when building a Fusion Reactor and progressing to ZPM, to name a few.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "For some reason, its fluid is a brilliant green - don't ask us why.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: Naquadah is a material originally from Stargate.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Don't you mean Stargatium?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naquadah Coils crank up the Heat Capacity to 7,200K.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "These coils are required for progressing to the second half of LuV and beyond.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: Well... not some \"juicy GregTech lore\", but a cool space fact, owing to the fact you're beating the Sun's temperature with your EBF. The surface temperature of the Sun is 5,800K. We're taking this as an effective temperature - i.e. the radiative temperature of the Sun as if it were a perfectly \"black body\". Check out the Stefan-Boltzmann law if you're interested in some crude astrophysics.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Oh, and the \"photosphere\" (the part of the sun we're talking about when we mention its surface)? It's 400 kilometres thick. That's 250 miles. Damn!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Hotter than the surface temperature of the Sun!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naquadah Coils",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "LuV Superconductors are necessary if you're eyeing a Fusion Reactor.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Don't ignore our warning here - you're gonna need a lot of them. Luckily, any Indium shortages will be solved by your Naquadah line.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Be glad these don't use pumps",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "LuV Superconductors",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Warning: Plasmas are extremely hot, with temperatures exceeding 30k Kelvin!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "However, this doesn't mean that a special type of pipe is required to transport plasma. Plasmas are treated slightly differently, and bend the rules a bit.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Fluid pipes that can handle plasmas, such as Tungsten, Naquadah, or Duranium can hold any plasma regardless of temperature. This means they won't melt under the heat of the plasma!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "WARNING: Plasmas in incompatible pipes will cause voiding, pipe burning, and disproportionately high rates of death. Be careful!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: This mechanic originates from GT6, just like the acids!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The stars are calling...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Fluid of the Stars",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Use this turbine to extract very large amounts of power from plasma!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Helium Plasma is the best fuel for its cost.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/aqueducts",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/aqueducts.json",
          "text": "Aqueducts are a tool used for moving Water around. Unlike Wooden Buckets, which are not able to transport water sources, Aqueducts are able to move source blocks horizontally any distance to their destination. They can be used to transport any type of water, including Fresh Water, Salt Water, and even Hot Spring Water.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Aqueducts",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {

          "text": "A singe aqueduct block can be crafted with some Bricks and Mortar.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Aqueducts",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "text": "In order to use an Aqueduct, you must simply connect them in a horizontal pattern, and place one end adjacent to either a Source or Falling water. After waiting a short while, water will begin to flow through the aqueduct network, and out the other end.
